#+TITLE: Manual tests: orgfdb query command options
* Paths
:PROPERTIES:
:VISIBILITY: folded
:END:

#+name: cargo-manifest
: ../../../../Cargo.toml

#+name: config-path
: ../Config.toml


* Setup

- Index the files under ~../org/~ with the manual-test configuration.
- Inspect complete JSON output manually where no exact output block is given.
- The source blocks assume the project noweb references ~cargo-manifest()~ and ~config-path()~ exist.

* Required ~--json~ mode

** Without ~--json~

Expected:

- exit code ~2~
- message contains ~query currently only supports --json~

#+begin_src shell :noweb yes :results raw :wrap SRC text
  set -o pipefail

  cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --config <<config-path()>> \
    '(headings (title "Query CLI heading" :exact t))' 2>&1
#+end_src

#+RESULTS:
#+begin_SRC text
query currently only supports --json
#+end_SRC

** With ~--json~

Expected:

- exit code ~0~
- valid JSON object
- matching heading appears

#+begin_src shell :noweb yes :results raw :wrap SRC js
  set -o pipefail

  cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --json --config <<config-path()>> \
    '(headings (title "Query CLI heading" :exact t))' \
  | jq .
#+end_src

* ~--output~

** Default output equals ~flat~

Expected:

- both commands have the same JSON shape
- matching semantics are identical

#+begin_src shell :noweb yes :results raw :wrap SRC text
  set -o pipefail

  default_output=$(cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --json --config <<config-path()>> \
    '(headings (file-name "query-cli-main.org" :exact t))')

  flat_output=$(cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --json --output flat --config <<config-path()>> \
    '(headings (file-name "query-cli-main.org" :exact t))')

  diff -u <(printf '%s\n' "$default_output" | jq -S .) <(printf '%s\n' "$flat_output" | jq -S .)
#+end_src

#+RESULTS:
#+begin_SRC text
#+end_SRC

- same matched objects as ~flat~

    '(headings (file-name "query-cli-main.org" :exact t))' \
  | jq .

** Repeated ~--output~ is invalid

Expected:

- Clap error
- exit code ~2~

#+begin_src shell :noweb yes :results raw :wrap SRC text
  set -o pipefail

  cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --json \
    --output flat --output outline --config <<config-path()>> \
    '(headings)' 2>&1
#+end_src

#+RESULTS:
#+begin_SRC text
error: the argument '--output <OUTPUT>' cannot be used multiple times

Usage: orgfdb query [OPTIONS] <QUERY>

For more information, try '--help'.

#+end_SRC

** Unknown ~--output~ value

Expected:

- Clap rejects the value
- exit code ~2~

#+begin_src shell :noweb yes :results raw :wrap SRC text
  set -o pipefail

  cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --json \
    --output tree --config <<config-path()>> \

#+end_src

** Duplicate includes are removed

Expected:

- output is identical to using ~--include path~ once

#+begin_src shell :noweb yes :results raw :wrap SRC text
  set -o pipefail

  once=$(cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --json --config <<config-path()>> \
    --include path \
    '(files (file-name "query-cli-main.org" :exact t))')

  duplicates=$(cargo run --quiet --manifest-path <<cargo-manifest()>> --bin orgfdb -- query --json --config <<config-path()>> \
    --include path,path --include path \
    '(files (file-name "query-cli-main.org" :exact t))')

  diff -u <(printf '%s\n' "$once" | jq -S .) <(printf '%s\n' "$duplicates" | jq -S .)
#+end_src

** Link ~source~ and ~target~

Expected:

- both include values are accepted
